--- Page 9 ---
<meta>This is the title page of the 1662 edition of Johannes de Monte-Snyders' work. It establishes the author's claim to have discovered a universal solvent capable of creating medicinal gold.</meta>

# TREATISE
## CONCERNING
# UNIVERSAL MEDICINE
## That is
## Concerning the Universal Medicine

->Specifically, how the same is to be found in the three<-
->kingdoms of minerals, animals, and vegetables /<-
->and how it is to be brought forth from them /<-
->through a special Universal Solvent <note>original: "Universal Menstruum"; in alchemy, a solvent used to dissolve solids</note>, which<-
->can open and close <note>a reference to the alchemical process of "solve et coagula" or dissolving and solidifying</note> / and can bring<-
->any metal into its First Matter <note>original: "Materiam primam"; the formless, primordial substance from which all things are made</note>.<-

->And<-

->How by this means the otherwise fixed,<-
->indestructible gold is to be brought into a true<-
->Potable Gold <note>original: "Aurum Potabile"; a legendary medicinal oil or liquid made from gold, believed to have miraculous healing powers</note> / which can never again<-
->be reduced back into a fixed<-
->body of gold.<-

->Written and published for the honor of God /<-
->and for the particular comfort and benefit<-
->of the human race<-

->By<-

->JOHANNES DE MONTE-SNYDERS <note>A significant 17th-century alchemist and writer known for his works on the transmutation of metals</note>.<-

->Printed at Frankfurt am Main /<-
->Published by Thomas Matthias Götzen /<-
->In the year 1662.<-

<meta>This page marks the beginning of the Preface (Vorrede). It introduces the author's moral and spiritual motivations for studying alchemy.</meta>

Preface.

<image-desc>A wide horizontal ornamental woodcut border featuring interlocking scrolls, circular patterns, and stylized floral motifs in a blackwork style.</image-desc>

## Kind Reader.

Since I have taken to heart the deceit and cunning with which the children of this world deal and are infected, and by which they likewise support themselves; and having taken the proverb deeply to heart, that **he who seeks to grow rich will not be innocent** <note>original Latin: *qui querit ditari, non erit innocens*; a common moral warning against greed</note>, my spirit was moved within me to think of ways and means by which I might live innocently and honestly, and be helpful to my neighbor. Therefore, I have applied myself to learn the knowledge of hidden, secret things in the <term>chemical science</term> <note>original: *Chymischen Scientz*; at this time, "chemistry" and "alchemy" were often used interchangeably to describe the study of natural secrets and the transformation of matter</note>, especially because I found that through this knowledge I could most safely serve and come to the aid of my soul and my neighbor without **offense against God** <note>original Latin: *offensa divina*</note>.

And since Almighty God, after great expenses incurred and through...

<sig>A ij</sig>
<meta>catchword: gestan</meta>

<summary>In this opening of the preface, the author explains that they turned to the study of alchemy as a moral alternative to the dishonest "cunning" of the worldly, seeking a way to help others without endangering their soul.</summary>
<keywords>preface, alchemy, chemical science, morality, neighborly love, greed, spiritual science</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the Preface. The previous page ended with the author describing how they sought a way to live honestly through the "chemical science" to help their neighbor after incurring great expenses.</meta>

Preface.

having endured great toil, God heard my prayer and enlightened me through His **Spirit of Truth**. Therefore, I have been moved by true **Christian love** for my neighbor not to hide this light, most graciously shared with me, under the bench or table, but to place it upon the table, so that it might shine all around and serve the people. For I am not minded to bury the <term>talent</term> <note>original German: *Pfund*; literally "pound," referring to the biblical Parable of the Talents (Matthew 25), where a servant is punished for burying the money/gifts entrusted to him by his master</note> I received out of envy, but rather to use it to perform the greatest possible good. 

And because this can be done quite effectively through my **Universal Medicine**, I have therefore allowed this <term>little work</term> <note>original Latin: *opusculum*</note> to be published in print. For the proverb states: **Your knowledge is nothing unless someone else knows that you know it** <note>original Latin: *scire tuum nihil est nisi te scire hoc sciat alter*; a famous line from the Roman poet Persius, emphasizing that knowledge should be shared to have value</note>. 

In this little work, however, the kind reader will be truthfully instructed and find that I have written not out of <term>mere opinion</term> <note>original: *Opinion*; in this context, it refers to guesswork or unproven theories found in books</note>, but from my own personal **experience**. This is done for the sake of those seekers who have gone astray, for the comfort of the <term>infirm</term> <note>original: *bresthafften*; an archaic term for those suffering from chronic illnesses or physical disabilities</note>, and for the benefit of the doctors—for all of whom I have intended such a Universal Medicine, namely a true <term>Potable Gold</term> <note>original Latin: *Aurum Potabile*; a legendary alchemical remedy consisting of gold refined into a liquid form, believed to be a "sovereign remedy" for all ailments</note>,

<summary>The author explains that after God answered their prayers and granted them enlightenment, they felt a religious duty to share their alchemical discoveries rather than keep them secret. They introduce their "Universal Medicine" or "Potable Gold" as a practical fruit of their personal experience intended to help both the sick and medical professionals.</summary>
<keywords>Christian love, parable of the talents, Universal Medicine, Aurum Potabile, potable gold, experience vs. opinion, medical charity, alchemy</keywords>

-ible, which can never again be reduced, and how to prepare it and keep it in supply; so that the needy shall henceforth always find the same with me. With this, I have already performed wondrous cures—namely in <term>consumption</term> <note>original Latin: *Phthisicis*; historically referring to wasting diseases, specifically pulmonary tuberculosis</note>, ailments of the head, and other feverish <term>diseases</term> <note>original Latin: *morbis*</note>, etc. I deem it unnecessary to recount these instances, for otherwise it might appear as if I sought vain glory; whereas I testify before God that I intend nothing else but His glory and the welfare of my neighbor. Otherwise, I would not have been able to make such a brief and clear <term>revelation</term> <note>original: *Offenbahrung*; in this context, the author refers to the disclosure of his "secret" medical knowledge or chemical processes</note>.

# ON UNIVERSAL MEDICINE,

## How a true lover of this high secret must be characterized and minded.

<image-desc>A large decorative drop cap 'A' set within a square frame filled with elaborate scrolling acanthus leaves and floral motifs.</image-desc>All those who wish to walk in this <term>Light of Nature</term> <note>original: *Liecht der Natur*; a central concept in Paracelsian philosophy referring to the divine wisdom and natural laws inherent in the physical world</note> must, above all, be of as pure and unstained a conscience as possible and possess a constant, holy <term>intention</term> <note>original: *Intention*; the practitioner's internal moral aim or spiritual focus</note>. They must also be tireless, steadfast, and furthermore, secretive to the highest degree and devoted to sobriety; for in a full, drunken, impure, and sullied heart, the Spirit of Wisdom—<term>namely</term> <note>original Latin: *nempè*</note> the Spirit of God—finds no rest nor remains. And outside of divine grace and the inspiration of the Holy Spirit, nothing can be hoped for in the chemical <term>science</term> <note>original: *Scienz*</note> that could be profitable to the soul or to one's neighbor. For this reason, a common proverb has also arisen: <term>Chemistry</term> <note>original: *Chymia*; in this period, the term referred to both alchemy and chemistry interchangeably</note> either finds a pious man or it makes him so. For the closer one comes to the knowledge of temporal...

Almighty God is the Alpha and the Omega <note>A reference to the Book of Revelation, signifying God as the beginning and the end of all existence</note>, the
beginning and the desired end, the **goal** <note>original Latin: *Scopus*; the target or mark one aims for</note>

Blessed, however, are those who grasp this

**illuminated** <note>original: *illuminirt*</note>, and led away from errors.
And should it happen that out of

like the prodigal son, repent of your

For know in the highest truth that it is
human to sin, and devilish to

hereby you should remember that to have
a good will is a holy sacrifice to
Almighty God.

is: For after the will finally follows the deed /

Therefore be prudent / keep the true light / which illuminates the **darkness of our spirit** <note>original German: *Finsternus vnsers Gemühts*</note> / constantly before your eyes / do as much good as you are able / and pray urgently for this grace / so the Most High will impart it to you / and open your eyes / so that you may clearly understand everything that I shall treat here for your true **instruction** <term>instruction: here meaning spiritual and practical guidance in the alchemical art; original: *Instruction*</term> : For the eternal Truth has commanded you to ask / and has also promised to give; **It is therefore a sin** <note>original Latin: *Nephas ergo est*</note>, **since God is inviolable truth and infallible salvation for the faithful, to doubt the efficacy of prayer** <note>original Latin: *cum Deus sit veritas inviolabilis & Salus infallibilis fidelibus dubitari de petitionis efficatia*</note>. To this, Christ the Lord also says: Hitherto you have asked for nothing in my name / whatever you shall ask in my name / that same thing the heavenly Father / who is in heaven / will give to you. And that for the honor and worthiness of His holy name / **because his name is invoked upon us** <note>original Latin: *quia invocatum est nomen ejus super nos*</note> , so terrible, lovable, and wonderful, which is praiseworthy for the salvation of all the faithful forever. Amen.

### Chapter One.
## On the Universal Solvent of all Metals and Minerals.
<note>original Latin title: *De Menstruo Vniversali omnium Metallorum & Mineralium*</note>

**Universal Solvent** <term>Universal Solvent: In alchemy, the "Menstruum Universale" or Alkahest, a hypothetical liquid capable of dissolving every other substance, including gold, into its primary matter.</term>

Here in this chapter, I will report and **instruct** <note>the text breaks off mid-word; original German: *vnterwei...*</note> the favorable and inclined lovers [of this Art] in the highest truth with a faithful heart...

Know / that two solutions <term>solutions: in alchemy, *solutiones* refers to the process of dissolving a solid into a liquid state; original: *Solutiones*</term> are found among the philosophers <note>the "philosophers" refers to the lineage of alchemists and natural scholars</note> / the first solution <note>original: *Solution*</note> is / when the matter is brought through the first matter into the first matter <note>original Latin: *materia per materiam primam in materiam primam*</note> / namely <note>original: *nempè*</note> into its three beginning things / such as into a dry water / which is called not only mercury <note>original: *Mercurius*</note> / but also sulfur <note>original: *Sulphur*</note>.

The dry solution <note>original: *Solution*</note> occurs through the magical elements in an open fire.

### Chapter Two.

## On the Elements in General.

The four elements are the first foundations <note>original Latin: *Fundamenta*</note> of all physical things / for from here all elementals <term>elementals: physical substances or beings composed of the four elements; original: *Elementata*</term> have emerged / and likewise were born from one mother into this world and given form / and when the elementals are corrupted <note>in this context, meaning "broken down" or "decayed"</note> / they resolve themselves once again into the elements : However, of these visible elements, none exists by itself <note>original Latin: *per se existens*</note> and pure, but [rather] mixed according to more or less <note>original Latin: *secundum magis & minus*</note> / and they are mutually transmutable into one another <note>original Latin: *suntque in se invicem transmutabilia*</note>; For out of the earth comes water / and again earth; out of the evaporated water comes air / and out of the condensation of the air comes water once more; Out of the ignited air comes fire / this fire becomes water through extinction <note>original Latin: *per exstinctionem*</note> / and this water becomes earth through great cold / and through heat into a stone or sulfur <term>sulfur: an alchemical principle representing combustibility and the soul; original: *Schwebel*</term> / all this happens through the elements' contrary and corresponding qualities; through the contrary qualities follows the antipathy <note>original Latin: *Antipathia*</note> or strife / but from the corresponding qualities <note>original Latin: *qualitatibus*</note> 

<summary>This page begins Chapter Two, defining the four elements as the fundamental building blocks of all physical matter. It explains the alchemical theory of elemental transmutation, where elements transition between states (fire, air, water, earth) based on their internal qualities and relationships of sympathy or strife.</summary>

<keywords>elements, transmutation, elementals, sulfur, antipathy, natural philosophy, reduction, solution</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the four elements, specifically focusing on the metaphysical process of destruction and regeneration. It builds upon the previous page's assertion that elements are mutually transmutable, explaining how they return to their source to take on new forms.</meta>

but <note>original: *tanquam per Simpathiam*</note> as if through sympathy <note>sympathy: in alchemy, a resonant attraction or hidden connection between substances</note> there occurs the assistance and the victory—that is, the destruction, which is the cause of a new generation <note>original Latin: *quae est causa novae generationis*; here, "generation" refers to the creation or begetting of a new substance</note>.

Therefore it is and remains the irrefutable, fundamental truth / that everything which is here below <note>original Latin: *quod est hic inferius*</note> is born of the elements / and is also to be transformed one into another / and that every elemental <term>elemental: a compound body or substance composed of the four elements; original: *Elementatū*</term> is destroyed by its own contrary and corresponding qualities / and thus is resolved back into its elements under another form: For destruction is nothing other than an abolition of the old forms / and also of the qualities into a new form / and a better essence.

Just as everything that is beneath the moon <note>meaning the sublunary or earthly realm, subject to change and decay</note> / has its origin in the coarse, visible elements: So too the elements have their origin from their superiors <note>original Latin: *à suis superioribus*; referring to higher spiritual or celestial powers</note>, for just as in the archetypal world <term>archetypal world: the divine realm of blueprints or ideas that precede physical reality; original: *Archetypo Mundo*</term> all things are in all things / so in this corporeal world all things are found in all things / though in different ways according to the nature of the recipients <note>original Latin: *modis tamen diversis pro natura suscipientium*</note> / and in this way the elements are not only in the heavenly bodies <note>original: *Corporibus*</note> / but also in the angels / etc. In the lower world <note>original Latin: *inferiori mundo*</note> they are coarse forms and material elements / but above they exist out of their own power and nature in an excellent heavenly way / and from this our coarse material elements have their beginning.

<meta>catchword: For [Dann]</meta>

<summary>This page explains the alchemical principle that the destruction of one form is necessary for the creation of a better one, rooted in the interconnectedness of the elements. It argues that earthly elements have their origins in higher celestial and archetypal realms, where they exist in a more refined, spiritual state.</summary>

13.

For fire has its origin *from the Sun, Mars,* and their *domiciles* <note>domiciles: in astrology, the specific zodiac signs where a planet is most powerful or "at home"</note>: Air *from Jupiter and Venus*: Water *from Saturn and Mercury*: Now the Earth has its beginning from the *Moon* and all of these; for the Earth is a receptacle <note>original Latin: *receptaculum*</note> of the heavenly influences, and in turn a mother <note>original German: *Gebärerin*, literally "one who gives birth"</note> of all *minerals* and *vegetables, etc.* Through the help of the elements—and these are, I say, in the higher realms *ideas of things to be produced, powers distributed among the intelligences <note>intelligences: celestial or angelic beings thought to govern the movements of the spheres</note>, virtues in the heavens, and in lower things coarser forms*—from these our matter is generated, and is endowed with contrary and *incompatible* qualities; from this follows the *corruption* <note>corruption: the alchemical process of decay or the breaking down of a substance's form so a new one can arise</note> of all *minerals, vegetables,* and *animals*; in the case of the latter, this *corruption* is called Death.

But according to how the *qualities accord* within an *elemental* <term>elemental: a physical body or substance composed of the four elements; original: *Elementato*</term>, they are accordingly *fixed* or *unfixed*—that is, difficult or easy to *dissolve.* Gold and silver are both *fixed bodies*, especially gold, in which the *qualities of the elements* agree and *harmonize* with such *harmony* that none holds the *preeminence* or the dominion. If gold were now to be *corrupted*, one *element* must gain the dominion and the rule against its enemy, to suppress or destroy it, and to *triumph totally*; this occurs through the help of corresponding qualities, through its own kind /

likewise, etc. For when an element is
fed, incited, ignited, and moved by the Solar body,

Therewith I will let it rest for this time, [ceasing] to **treat** <note>original: *tractiren*</note> of the elements **in general** <note>original: *in genere*</note>, and turn myself to the Fiery Magical Element; which, because it is threefold or of three kinds, I shall explain thoroughly in what follows.

### Chapter Three.
# On the Magical Elements,
### That is, of the Triple Fire of the Ancient Sages.

Neither the **philosophers** <note>original: *Philosophi*; referring to natural philosophers or alchemists</note> nor **chemists** <note>original: *Chymici*</note> can for all eternity bring about any true, right, irreducible **solution** <note>original: *Solution*; the process of dissolving a solid into a liquid state</note> without these three secret fires, of which the first fire must bring the metal into flux.

The second, however, has a **sympathy** <note>original: *Simpathiam*; a natural attraction or resonance between two substances</note> with the metallic fire; and although this is likewise twofold or double, I will nevertheless take it as single, and not regard that they have a **contrary nature** <note>original: *contrariam naturam*</note> in their **qualities** <note>original: *qualitatibus*</note>, but only **attend** <note>original: *attendiren*</note> to the fact that they produce an **effect** <note>original: *effectum*</note> in my undertaking. This second fire now ignites the metallic **sulfur** <term>Sulfur: the active, combustible principle in alchemy, responsible for color and growth; Latin: *Sulphur*</term> and increases the **element of fire** <note>original: *Elementum Ignis*</note> within the metallic body.

<summary>This page introduces the "Triple Fire" of alchemy, asserting that no true chemical dissolution is possible without these three secret fires. It begins to describe how the first fire creates flux and the second fire ignites the internal sulfur of a metal.</summary>
<keywords>triple fire, alchemical solution, sulfur, metallic body, improvement of metals, magical elements</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the "Triple Fire" of the ancient sages, moving from the second fire to the third fire, which is described as the foundational element of the alchemical work.</meta>

16.

The third fire is a cold metallic fire, almost to be compared to a **Mercury** <note>original: *Mercurio*; in alchemy, Mercury represents the principle of fluidity, volatility, and the "spirit" of a substance</note>; for it wanders through the metal as a spirit and facilitates that the sympathetic fire might permeate it **entirely** <note>original: *in toto*</note> and can ignite the **soul** <note>original: *animam*</note> at all ends. It makes the metal **porous** <note>original: *poro os*; likely a transcription or derivation of *porosum*</note>, opens and closes [it], and is the beginning and the end, the first and last key. **In short** <note>original: *in summa*</note>, it is the **foundation of the entire work** <note>original: *fundamentum totius operis*</note>, for it is also a **vehicle** <note>original: *vehiculum*</note> of the sympathetic fire, and is destroyed and corrupted by this fire.

This comes from **antipathy and sympathy** <note>original: *Antipathia & Simpathia*; the natural forces of repulsion and attraction between substances</note>, for the burning fire is related to the cold metallic fire in its lineage, and it is a double fire, **as I said above** <note>original: *uti superius dixi*</note>, to be compared and attributed to **Sulphur** <note>original: *Sulphure*</note> and **Mercury** <note>original: *Mercurio*</note>, or the serpent itself. It is called **Sulphur and Nitre** <note>original: *Sulphur & Nitrum*</note> by the philosophers, and by this, the philosophers have always understood the double sympathetic fire, because it carries two contrary **qualities** <note>original: *qualitates*</note> with it: for it is cold and warm, moist and dry. Therefore, it is to be considered the true **universal solvent** <note>original: *Menstruum Universale*</note> and the **prime matter** <note>original: *Materia prima*; the original, undifferentiated substance from which all things are created</note> itself, because here in this **dry liquid** <note>original: *liquori sicco*; an alchemical paradox describing a substance that flows like water but does not wet the hands</note>, from which the **mercury of the philosophers** <note>original: *Mercurius Philosophorum*</note> is prepared, the metals dissolve readily and easily without their **center** <note>original: *Centrum*</note> being displaced.

The **radical solution** <note>original: *Radical Solution*; a total dissolution that breaks a substance down to its very "root" or essence</note> must therefore be of such a nature that it can by no means [be restored] to its former [form]...

<meta>catchword: form [stalt]</meta>

### Chapter Four.

## Concerning the Destruction of Metals, and Reduction into the Three Principles, the Things.
<term>Three Principles|Known in Latin as the *Tria Prima* (Salt, Sulfur, and Mercury), these were the foundational components Paracelsus believed made up all material things.</term>

Without corruption <note>In alchemy, "corruption" or "putrefaction" refers to the necessary breakdown or "death" of a substance before it can be reborn or purified into a higher state.</note> there is no resurrection / 
nor glorification or multiplication to be hoped 
for / for corruption is the highest degree / 
and the first beginning of a new generation and 
multiplication / therefore we wish to treat here 
of the most principal point / namely of the 
destruction and reduction into **prime matter.** 
<note>original: *materiam primam.* This refers to the original, formless state of matter that alchemists sought to return substances to before rebuilding them.</note>

This destruction and corruption / however / can 
happen in no other way than solely with 
this thing / from which the matter came and was 
born / and into which the same shall go again / and in 
every thing is the cause of its birth / 
and also the cause of its destruction / this 
latter cause the chemists <note>original: *Chymici*.</note> must especially 
observe.

## On the Nature and Property of the First Principle, **Mercury**. <note>original Latin: *Mercurii*</note>

**The** common **Mercury of Metals** <note>original Latin: *Mercurius Metallorum*</note> is of a hot **temperament** <note>original: *Complexion*; in early medicine and alchemy, this refers to the balance of bodily humors or qualities.</note>; its true quality is **moisture** and **warmth** / it therefore has a **concordance** <note>original: *Concordanz*</note> with the air / and the **philosophers** <note>original: *Philosophi*</note> understand by the element of air **mercury** itself / because of the great **affinity** <note>original: *Affinitet*</note> it has with the air / it is by nature volatile / spiritual / and corpore- <note>The word is cut off by the catchword "lisch," forming "corporeal."</note>

## On the Property of the Third Principle, namely Salt.

Salt is the *preservation* <note>original: *Conservation*</note>, constancy, and *hardening* <note>original: *induration*; the process of making a substance solid or resistant.</note> of all things; it preserves especially against *decay* <note>original: *Corruption*; the chemical breakdown or rotting of a substance.</note>. For this reason, nature has joined this to all *fixed elemental beings* <note>original: *fixen Elementatis*; substances composed of the elements that have reached a stable state.</note> or permanent creatures as a companion and protector. It is the *principle* <note>original: *principium*; one of the three fundamental building blocks of matter in alchemical theory (Salt, Sulfur, and Mercury).</note> most resistant to fire, which must maintain the other two in their essence and state. Salt is of an earthly nature.

The *Earth of the Wise* is salt; it alone is indestructible, incombustible, and invincible. Because of its mighty and powerful virtues, the ancient *philosophers* <note>original: *Philosophi*; referring here to alchemists and natural philosophers.</note> compared salt to Divine Majesty, ascribed a divine power to it, and called it an *Electrum* <note>In alchemy, this term often refers to a highly potent substance or a "living" metal alloy believed to have celestial powers.</note>. If God Almighty had not ordained this principle as a protection for the two weaker principles, *Sulfur and Mercury* <note>original Latin: *Sulphuris & Mercurii*</note>, then His creatures would have had to perish and would have been, as it were, *reduced to nothing* <note>original Latin: *quasi in nihilum reduciret worden*</note>. It is the fundamental truth: if God Almighty had not created salt, the *Philosophers’ Stone* <note>original Latin: *Lapis Philosophorum*; the legendary substance capable of turning base metals into gold and granting long life.</note> could not be made. For the preservation and *fixation* <note>original: *Figirung*; the alchemical process of making a volatile substance stable and solid.</note> of all things is worked by the

### *Chapter Eight.*

## Whether the *Universal Medicine* <note>A legendary substance, often equated with the Philosopher's Stone, capable of curing all diseases and perfecting base metals.</note> is to be found in the seven *stars* <note>original Latin: *Astris*; here referring to the seven metals of antiquity, each associated with a celestial body (Gold/Sun, Silver/Moon, etc.).</note>, that is, in the seven metals, or rather in Gold and the *prime matter* <note>original Latin: *materia prima*; the original, formless substance from which all matter was believed to have been created.</note>.

All *philosophers* <note>Referring here to alchemists and natural philosophers of the past.</note> who have written of the *Universal Medicine* and *prime matter* before now and up to the present day are not only of differing and contradictory opinions, but are also so *obscure* <note>Intentionally difficult to understand or vaguely written to protect alchemical secrets.</note> that no firm ground can be found in their words. At times, it appears as if they meant a common *Mercury*, a common *Saturn*,

...[Sa]turn <note>Likely "Saturnum" (Lead), continuing from the previous page.</note>, but sometimes it seems as if they intended to indicate *saltpeter* <term>Nitrum</term> or *vitriol* <term>Vitriolum</term> alone; thus they use this or that name, though they mean something quite different by it. There have been few *philosophers* <note>alchemists</note> who did not always describe and signify the matter *metaphorically*, or in some other concealed manner. Indeed, what is more, they treat the medicinal effects, virtues, and powers more than the thing itself—how one might rightly recognize it, or where it may be found, and how, after gaining knowledge of the *material* <term>materia</term> (without which nothing can be accomplished), one should proceed in the shortest and safest way. Concerning this, it is said: *for by concealing the material, they have only revealed the effect.* <note>original Latin: *materiam enim celantes solum effectum patefecerunt.*</note>

I, however, will provide here such clear and certain instruction as has never happened before me. For I shall deal clearly not with the powers, but much more with the *material* <term>materia</term> itself, and how it is to be brought *to a medicinal state* <note>original Latin: *ad medicinam*</note>, using simple words and a humble *style*. I do this in consideration of the fact that almost all philosophical books are full of praise, and until now there has been no doubt regarding the *virtues* <term>Virtutibus; the inherent healing powers or properties of a substance</term>, but rather the *material* has been the cause of doubt.

Therefore, I entrust to your conscience once again that our *material* <term>materia</term> is comparable to the whole world and the soil of the earth, *namely as far as its virtues are concerned, etc.* <note>original Latin: *nempè quoad virtutes, &c.*</note> For here is an attractive...

### Cap. Nonum. <note>Chapter Nine</note>

# Of the Nature and Property of the Earthly Planet Saturn.
## <note>In alchemical tradition, the planet Saturn corresponds to the metal Lead.</note>

->*Chapter Eleven.*<-

## On the Nature and Property of Mars, which is Iron.

The philosophers say: **He who is ignorant of Mars does not know the art** <note>original: *Qui ignorat Martem, non novit artem*</note>. That is to say, whoever does not know or recognize the **qualities** <note>original: *qualitates*</note> of iron is not an expert in the art. Furthermore, whoever does not know how one should destroy Mars <note>Iron</note> through that which lies hidden within himself, and likewise bring it into its **prime matter** <note>original: *materiam primam*</note>, the invincible Martial Prince of War will provide him no assistance in his highest needs, nor in his **chemical undertakings** <term>Chymischen Anschlägen; the practical operations or experimental plans of the alchemist</term>.

## Chapter *Twelve.*

# On the Nature and Property of *Sol*, that is,
# of Gold.

Gold is the purest, the most fixed, and the most perfect metal, in which the **qualities of the Elements** <note>original: "qualitates Elementorum"</note> are most perfectly **in concord**. And although one might wish to **judge** <note>original: "iudiciren"</note> from the high-glancing color of gold that the **Sulfur** within the gold surpasses the other **Principles**, or the **Elements of Water and Earth**, experience shows the right and fundamental truth only when gold is brought back through **Anatomy** <term>Anatomia|In an alchemical context, this refers to the chemical "dissection" or decomposition of a substance into its constituent parts</term> by the power of the magical elements to what it was before: namely, **Mercury, Sulfur, and Salt** <note>The "Tria Prima" or three primary substances that Paracelsian alchemy taught were the base of all matter</note>. ## On the Nature and Properties of Fugitive Mercury.
<note>original: "Fugitivi Mercurii." Quicksilver was called "fugitive" because it easily evaporates or "flees" when exposed to heat.</note>

The ancient philosophers placed **Mercury** <term>Mercurius</term> only on the second rank of honor, which is highly surprising, since Mercury is by nature proud and haughty; he even raises himself above all the **stars** <term>Astra</term> <note>In alchemy, "stars" often refers to the internal virtues or planetary spirits within terrestrial matter.</note> to show that his seat should rightly be at the top and that the highest honor is due to him, because within him the highest **secret** <term>Arcanum</term> <note>A profound mystery or a "great secret" of nature, usually referring to a powerful medicinal essence.</note> of human health is to be found.

He is otherwise companionable to all the stars because of the **astral seed**. The planet with which he conjoins is strengthened in its own nature: if that planet is malicious, it becomes even more evil; but if it is good, it is improved and strengthened in its purpose through the union with Mercury. In short, Mercury is like a hypocrite or a politician, who prefers to offend no one but instead agrees with everyone.

Otherwise, he is by nature hot and fiery in his entire being. The **metallic fire** <term>Metallisch Feuer</term> is so powerful within Mercury that his body must therefore remain in a constant state of flow <note>A reference to the fact that mercury is the only metal that remains liquid at room temperature.</note>. And although this Mercury possesses many powerful **medicinal powers** <term>Medicinalische Kräfften</term> and virtues, it is

[this] happen through a *sympathetic medicine* <note>original: "Sympathica medicina"; a branch of healing based on the Hermetic belief that objects sharing a common nature or celestial governor can influence one another regardless of distance</note>. This must happen through a sympathetic medicine <term>sympathetic medicine (Sympathica medicina); a historical medical theory that substances with similar natures or celestial correspondences can cure one another</term>; but this medicine itself must first be liberated and purged of all infection <note>original: "Infection"</note> which it has drawn to itself from its superior <note>original: "à suo superiori"; meaning from the celestial or planetary influences above</note>, and it must stand in the closest equal or even superior degree with the first cause <note>original: "causa prima"</note>.

### *Chapter Sixteen.*

# Of the Nature and Property of Precious Stones / how to bring them to the high Medicine / through the *Universal Solvent of the Philosophers.* <term>Menstruum Universale Philosophorum; the "universal solvent" or "philosophers' menstruum," a substance believed to dissolve any matter into its primary components</term>

Precious stones have their beginning and origin from the Astral *First Mover* <term>Primo mobili; in medieval cosmology, the "First Mover" is the outermost sphere that gives motion to the universe</term>, just as much as the highest and lowest metals; therefore, in their colors, sulfurs <term>sulphuren; the alchemical "soul" or principle of color and combustibility</term>, and other properties, they correspond with the seven *higher and lower stars* <note>original Latin: "Astris superioribus & inferioribus"; referring to the planets in the heavens and their corresponding metals within the earth</note> / and perform their work / once they have first been released from their earthly bonds and the shackles of the earthly curse / and have entered into their three beginning things <term>tria prima; the three primary principles of Salt, Sulfur, and Mercury which alchemists believed composed all matter</term> / which shall occur through the *Universal Solvent*.

### *Simple Alphabet.*
<note>original Latin: *Alphabeta simplici*</note>

♄ **Lead**; is also called the Black Wolf, **Death** <note>original Latin: *Mors*</note>, and Gold.

Therefore, know first of all that all things are formed and made into this or that **body** <note>original: *Leib*; referring to physical manifestation</note> out of three essences, through an **astral impression** <term>Astral Impression</term> <note>the influence or "imprint" cast by celestial bodies onto earthly matter</note> and **elementary action** <note>the physical interaction and movement of the four elements</note>.

The elements are found in the metals, and not the metals in the **elements** <note>original: *Elementis*</note>: for the **metallic seed** <note>the spiritual or internal "blueprint" that allows a metal to grow or transform</note> is not as far removed as some fools have imagined.

And although the elements must work together in the formation of every **body** <note>original: *Corpus*</note>, they are nonetheless not the Philosophical **First Matter** <term>Materia Prima</term> <note>the original, undifferentiated substance from which all things were created</note>, or the **Astral Seed** <note>original: *Semen Astrale*; the celestial spark or life-force that directs growth</note>; they are, however, a cause of **generation** and **corruption** <note>original: *Generation und Corruption*; the cycle of birth/creation and decay/destruction</note>, and **the decay of one is the birth of another** <note>original: *Corruptio unius est Generatio alterius*; a fundamental law of nature in alchemy where the breaking down of one substance provides the material for a new one</note>.

The ancient philosophers are of the opinion that all things were created out of hate or love, that is, **out of sympathy and antipathy** <note>original: *ex Sympathia & Antipathia*</note>, as if from two opposing qualities of the elements, which perform this action out of a heavenly influence, and especially an **astral impression**, etc. Now the elements are incorporated into the metals and the minerals; therefore, these also possess their **qualities** <note>original: *qualitates*</note>.

There are only two of these contrary qualities through which everything is generated and born.

Fire is hot and dry; its **opposite** <note>original: *contrarium*</note> is the cold, moist **element** <note>original: *Elementum*</note> of **water** <term>▽</term> <note>original: *aquæ*; the downward-pointing triangle ▽ is the alchemical symbol for the element of Water</note>.

The Air is moist and warm; its **opposite** is the dry, cold Earth: And because no **bo-**

<sig>G 4</sig>

**body** <note>original: *pus*, likely the completion of *Corpus* from the previous page</note> is to be found without the four elements, and the elements are incorporated in all things; therefore, there is a **contrariety** <term>Contrarietet</term> <note>a state of opposition or conflict between internal forces</note> in every **elemental thing** <term>Elementato</term> <note>any physical object composed of the four elements</note>. For these reasons, all things are transient and destructible: for when two contrary things exist **in one subject** <note>original: *in uno subiecto*; meaning within a single physical entity</note>, there is an everlasting strife, until finally one part receives help from its external element; then the opposite must immediately yield and give place to the stronger, and grant the **victory** <note>original: *Victori*</note>. From this arises a **corruption** <term>Corruption</term> <note>the decay or breaking down of a substance</note> or separation of the entire being, so that a new form lies before our eyes, which has been **generated** through that very corruption.

The elements of the metals are their three primary **matrices** <term>Matrices</term> <note>literally "wombs"; the foundational sources or origins of metallic matter</note>: **Mercury**, **Sulphur**, and **Salt** <note>original: *Mercurius, Sulphur, & Sal*; known as the *Tria Prima* or three beginnings in alchemy</note>. These exist as **contraries** <note>original: *Contraria*</note> in perpetual strife and enmity. For **Mercury** carries enmity **as an airy spirit** <note>original: *tanquam Spiritus Aereus*</note> against the Salt, which represents the Earth; and the hot **Sulphur**, acting **as fire** <note>original: *tanquam Ignis*</note>, is hostile to the Water—**namely** <note>original: *nempè*</note>, it is opposed to the moisture of the **Mercury**, for this is the "water" of the metals. It represents two elements: Air and Water.

These aforementioned three **matrices** or metallic elements receive their nourishment from our material, visible, and tangible elements. They fight in their mortal house for so long, until the ordained time when one element, through external help, becomes too strong for the other—

have; it is nevertheless for the best of those who are chosen for it and are inclined to do good works.

One can also certainly destroy **metals** by increasing the **salt**, using a **mineral** and **tartaric salt** <term>Saltz</term> <note>Salts derived from minerals or cream of tartar; these were used as flux or solvents in alchemy</note>, as I have cited at length previously; however, it does not serve to obtain the **metallic sulfur** <term>Schwebel</term> <note>original: *Metallischen Schwebels*; the combustible, "soul" principle of a metal</note>, which is the soul of all **metals**. Therefore, it must first be captured with great care by its own kind—**namely** <note>original: *nempè*</note> through the two contrary burning fires, which are called ***Sulphur*** and ***Nitrum*** <term>Nitrum</term> <note>niter or saltpeter</note>, and are sometimes understood **under a single name** <note>original: *sub uno nomine*</note>.

But so that you may also have knowledge of the cold **metallic fire**, know that it is nothing other than the **Mercury of Saturn** <note>original: *Mercurius Saturni*; typically refers to the essential liquid or "mercury" extracted from lead</note>, which **amalgamates** <note>original: *peramalgamirt*; to combine or alloy with mercury</note> with the **metals** and, with the help of the aforementioned fiery double **element** <note>original: *Elemens*</note>, allows itself to be **calcined** <note>original: *vercalciniren*; the process of heating a substance to high temperatures to reduce it to a powder or "ash"</note> in the fire.

Now you have received a clear—yes, beyond all measure clear—report from me as to how all **metals** were born **from Sulfur and Mercury** <note>original: *ex Sulphure & Mercurio*</note> through an **astral sidereal imagination** <note>The belief that the stars and planets "imprint" their qualities onto earthly matter during its formation</note> and **elemental** effect. Accordingly, the **metals** are also nourished by the **elements**, live, and die away again—that is, they must be brought back into their first essence.

That the **metals** cannot do without the **elements**, and that the **philosophers** <note>original: *Philosophi*; referring here to alchemists</note> [reckon] only two **elements**...

[aug]mentation completed / etc. And it is a **Universal Medicine** in all illnesses / whoever partakes of it / receives a renewal of his **Spirit** / and a strengthening of all his departed forces.

And whoever wishes to know / how and when he should apply this medicine / for the sake of not delaying myself further / I will refer him to the *books of the Philosophers* <note>original: *Philosophorum libros*</note> / and remain with my *intended purpose* <note>original: *Proposito*</note>.

The favorable reader will without doubt be greatly amazed by my clear and thorough report / which I have provided in the *repetition* <note>original: *Reperition*; likely referring to the summary or recapitulation of the process</note> / especially concerning the cold *Mercury of Saturn* <note>original: *Mercurii Saturni*; "Saturn" is the alchemical name for lead, so this refers to a liquid essence extracted from lead</note>, and concerning the *Hellish Magical Fiery Element* / without which nothing fruitful can be accomplished in the whole of *Chemical Science* <note>original: *Chymischen Scientz*</note> / but to him this shall serve as an answer / that although I have brought things to light for the experienced far too clearly / and even more distinctly than any *Philosopher* <note>original: *Philosophus*</note> has ever written: yet there is still one small thing / which I have withheld from them / namely how one should make from an unmolten, brittle *Mineral Saturn* <note>original: *Mineralischen Saturno*; raw lead ore</note> such a highly precious *Mercury* / and how the same is also to be brought into a red *Spirit* <note>original: *Spiritum*</note>. This can only happen through a single way and means / concerning which I have indeed lit a light earlier on;

Secondly, I have not only partially hidden the preparation of the cold <term>Metallic Fire</term> <note>original: *Metallischen Fewrs*; in alchemy, "fire" often refers to a corrosive or transformative liquid that "burns" or dissolves metals without heat.</note>, but also the preparation of the <term>Fiery Magical Key</term> <note>original: *Fewrigen Magischen Schlüssels*</note>, which, as you have often understood, is <term>prepared</term> <note>original: *præparirt*</note> from two contrary, clashing fires, namely from <term>Sulfur</term> <note>original: *Sulphur*</note> and <term>Glass</term> <note>original: *vitrum*; likely referring here to vitriol or a glass-like mineral state</note>, which, although they may both be called hellish clashing fires, must nevertheless be made one to such an extent that they both <term>perform a single effect</term> <note>original: *effectum præstiren*</note>.

But how this union occurs, for that ask God for the enlightenment of your mind; I have done enough for my part, and have made more than too much manifest in this little book: The reason is the love of my neighbor, whom I would gladly encourage with my meager experience and bring onto the right path. For what does my science help me? What helps me the buried <term>Talent</term> <note>original: *Talentum*; a reference to the Biblical parable of the talents, where a servant is punished for burying his master's money rather than investing it; here, it refers to the author's duty to share his knowledge.</note>? <meta>This page continues from page 116, where the author discussed the necessity of keeping certain alchemical preparations secret from those who might misuse them, specifically mentioning a hidden "Metallic Fire."</meta>

Secondly, it is also right and fair that all that is good should be shared—specifically among those who have bound and sworn themselves to the <term>Philosophers</term> <note>original: *Philosophis*; here referring to the community of alchemists and seekers of natural wisdom.</note> and are numbered among them. But to the others, those who are on the outside, everything happens through parables, so that they might hear and yet not understand, see and yet not comprehend. One should not cast pearls before swine, for they will only defile them and hold them in no regard, and so forth.

One thing which could cause you great confusion <term>by reason of</term> <note>original: *ratione*</note> the two <term>Saturns</term> <note>original: *Saturni*; the planetary name for lead</note> or <term>Mercuries</term> <note>original: *Mercurii*</note>, I shall not pass over here; therefore, pay special attention to my report.

Previously, you understood that from the <term>Moon</term> <note>original: *Lunâ*; the alchemical name for silver</note>, and also from the cold, Capricornian <term>Saturn</term> <note>original: *Saturno*; lead, which was associated with the zodiac sign Capricorn</note>, a <term>Mercury</term> <note>original: *Mercurius*</note> is to be made, and that this is especially necessary for the Work of the Sages.

<term>Secondly</term> <note>original: *Secundò*</note>, that from the <term>Ore of Venus</term> <note>original: *Minera Veneris*; copper ore</note> a <term>Solar Mercury</term> <note>original: *Solarischen Mercurium*</note> is likewise to be made, which is endowed with a solar sulfur; for this reason, I called it at that time the <term>Mercury of the Sun</term> <note>original: *Mercurium Solis*</note>, because it is to be used <term>for the generation of the Sun</term> <note>original: *ad generationem Solis*; the alchemical process of creating or perfecting gold</note>.

Thirdly, that despite this, I have now used the <term>Mercury of Saturn</term> <note>original: *Mercurium Saturni*</note> in the <term>destruction of gold</term> <note>original: *Destruction des Goldes*; the chemical dissolution or breaking down of gold into its base components</note>...

...had used; he should understand that the red *Mercury* <note>original: *Mercurius*</note> is also to be used in the *white Lunar Tincture* <note>original: *Lunarischen weissen Tinctur*; an alchemical preparation associated with silver (the Moon) used to "tinge" or transmute base metals.</note> with great and better benefit. And just as one cannot *proceed from one extreme* <note>original: *ab uno extremo... schreiten*</note> to the other *extreme* <note>original: *extremo*</note> without a middle step, so too can one arrive at no red tincture unless one has first obtained the white. Thus the favorable reader may now resolve his own *doubt* <note>original: *dubium*</note> and consider that I have written here not of the *Greatest Universal* <note>original: *Universali Generalissimo*; likely referring to the ultimate Philosopher's Stone capable of all transmutations and healings.</note>, but only of a simple *Universal*.

The *Greatest Universal* is born from a double *Mercury*, as I mentioned before, and is animated and *fermented* with a *Solar Sulfur* <note>original: *Solarischen Sulphur*; sulfur associated with the Sun or gold, representing the active, masculine principle.</note>, also *fixed* <note>original: *figirt*; the process of making a volatile substance stable so it can withstand fire.</note> with the constant *Salt of Gold*, and further *augmented infinitely* <note>original: *in infinitum... augmentirt*</note> in *quantity and quality* <note>original: *quantitate & qualitate*</note> through two other *Sulfurs*. The *quality*, as a life-force, is fed by the *Sulfur*; the *quantity* is augmented by the aforementioned *Mercury*, which carries within it not only the nature and power of the *Venereal property* <note>original: *Venerischen Eygenschafft*; properties associated with the planet Venus, representing copper.</note>, but also the nature of mineral, cold *Saturn* <note>original: *Saturni*; representing lead.</note>. For this reason, it is compared to a double *Mercury* and is called a twofold *Mercury*, and all qualities are attributed to it; *for it has the quality of Venus, by reason of* <note>original: *nam habet qualitatem Veneris, ratione*</note> the hot *Sulfur*; *but coldness by reason of Saturn* <note>original: *Frigiditatem autem ratione Saturni*</note>.

<meta>This page continues from page 116, concluding the discussion on the preparation of the double Mercury and the "Greatest Universal" tincture. The author shifts from technical instructions to a personal appeal to the reader, emphasizing the practical validity of his work before transitioning away from mineral-based alchemy.</meta>

The kindhearted, inclined lover <note>The "lover of the art" or "philosopher" was the standard term for a dedicated student of alchemy.</note> should let go of all doubt and disbelief, and believe in my *experience* <note>original: *Experientz*</note> in truth. You may do whatever you wish, but you will not be able to accuse me of any lies; for I have not gathered this from other books in order to perhaps make myself a famous name, but have experienced it through <term>manual labor</term> <note>original: *Handt-Arbeit*; this refers to actual physical practice in the laboratory (the "work of the hands") rather than just theoretical study.</note>, and subsequently I understood what I should have known before. To the inclined lover, however, is granted the thorough explanation of the *Secret* <note>original: *Geheimnuss*</note> of all Sages, which I indeed described earlier, but have now composed in this *repetition* <note>original: *Wiederholung*</note> in the shortest possible manner, so that you should *repeat* this *repetition* by *reading* <note>original: *legendo*</note> often and for a long time, until you have learned everything internal by heart.

And because I know that this my *little treatise* <note>original: *Tractätlein*</note> cannot be *attacked* <note>original: *impugnirt*; to be refuted or challenged by argument.</note>, it will be bought up in all places—partly out of fear, partly out of hatred and envy—so that it will come into the hands of few. I, however, will take my own meaning from it, and know what I have done and what I should do even further. Meanwhile, I wish herewith to have made an end to my *repetition* *regarding* <note>original: *ratione*</note> the two magical *elements*, the <term>solvent</term> <note>original: *Menstrui*; a solvent used to dissolve solids, often considered the "blood" of the alchemical process.</note> *or* <note>original: *sivê*</note> *doubled mercury* <note>original: *Mercurii duplicati*</note>, and to cease *treating* of the *medicine* that consists in the mineral kingdom.

<meta>This page concludes the author's summary or "repetition" of his alchemical theory. Having previously discussed the preparation of the double Mercury, he here identifies the foundational components of the work—the prime matter and the universal solvent—before concluding this section of the treatise with a solemn prayer for the reader’s success and the protection of these secrets from the unworthy.</meta>

Therefore, be content with the magical elements, with the *solvent* <note>original: *Menstruo*; in alchemy, a "menstruum" is a solvent capable of dissolving solids, essential for the process of transmutation.</note> and the *universal medicine*, with the *prime matter* <note>original: *Materiâ Primâ*; the original, undifferentiated substance from which all metals and things are created.</note>, through which every metal can be reduced to its three beginning principles <note>referring to the *Tria Prima*: Salt, Sulfur, and Mercury.</note> and through which the true *potable gold* <note>original: *Aurum Potabile*; a medicinal elixir made from gold, believed to have life-prolonging and curative properties.</note> must be made. The *prime matter* is represented to you in German words, as are both elements or *fiery keys* <note>original: *Fewrige Schlüssel*; symbolic tools or reagents used to "unlock" the hidden powers within the matter.</note>, so clearly—yes, so very clearly—and demonstrated *to the eye* <note>original: *ad Oculum*; visibly or through direct evidence.</note> that you will not desire it to be any clearer.

The Eternal, Merciful Creator of Heaven and Earth, the First and the Last, who holds the keys of Heaven and Hell, may He enlighten the minds and reason of the worthy and humble, and so shut and lock away the understanding and will of the godless, the proud, and the greedy, so that no evil may arise from this, my sincere intention, but rather that all good must be worked through it, to the honor and praise of His Holy Name, for the benefit of our neighbor, and for the salvation and bliss of our own souls. Amen.

<meta>This page serves as a concluding notice or advertisement following the theoretical and practical instructions provided on the previous page. The author, having just finished explaining the "fiery keys" and the "prime matter" required for the universal medicine, now addresses the logistical reality of how the reader might actually acquire these substances or contact him.</meta>

# Notice to the Reader.

Kind reader. Since in my previous [writings] I have made one or another obligation <note>original: *Obligation*; here referring to a formal promise or commitment to provide information or services.</note> that might benefit and serve the kindhearted reader, and have otherwise promised to arrange for the preparation and production of the true irreducible **potable gold** <note>original: *Aurum Potabile irreducibile*; an alchemical liquid gold that was believed to be "irreducible," meaning it could not be turned back into common metal because it had been spiritually transformed into a medicine.</note>, so that those who have need of it as a **universal medicine** <note>original: *Universal Medicin*; a "cure-all" panacea sought by alchemists to heal all diseases and prolong life.</note> might seek it and obtain it.

And as I am then inclined, for particular and significant reasons, to settle in a specific place, I should in the meantime—in order to remain true to my offering <note>original: *Oblation*.</note> and promise—fairly inform the kind reader where I may be found; for in the absence of this information, the offering and promises would be powerless and take on the appearance of a mere **boast** <note>original: *Jactation*; from the Latin *iactatio*, meaning a vain or ostentatious display.</note>.

Therefore, let the kindhearted reader be informed herewith that, although I will no longer be found in Cologne <note>original: *Cöllen*.</note>, where I stayed for a considerable time, he may nevertheless receive news of my absence <note>possibly meaning news of his current location during his absence from Cologne</note> from **Jacob Hansen**, whose name is very well known at the **post offices** <note>original: *Post-Comptoiren*; these were the central hubs for mail and communication in the 17th and 18th centuries.</note> there, and also to many enthusiasts <note>original: *Liebhaberen*; literally "lovers" of the art, referring to fellow alchemists or amateur practitioners.</note> in various places.

<meta>catchword: Die</meta>

<summary>In this notice, the author informs the reader that he is moving from Cologne but remains committed to his promise of providing the "potable gold" medicine. He directs interested readers to contact a certain Jacob Hansen at the Cologne post offices to find his new location.</summary>
<keywords>potable gold, universal medicine, Cologne, Jacob Hansen, post offices, alchemical medicine, logistics</keywords>

--- Page 147 ---
<meta>This page continues the author's concluding remarks from the previous page, where he explained his departure from Cologne. He now transitions from personal logistics to the final theoretical justification for his "Potable Gold" and why it is superior to common metallic gold.</meta>

But the reason why I wish to remain unknown is that I am intent on entering into retirement. In the meantime, the *Potable Gold* <note>original: *Aurum Potabile*; a medicinal liquid form of gold believed to have life-prolonging properties.</note> will be available in Cologne from the aforementioned *Jacob Hanssen*; it has been prepared from the true **Lion’s Blood** <note>original: *Löwenbluht*; a highly symbolic alchemical term usually referring to a secret solvent or a specific state of treated antimony or mercury used to "dissolve" gold.</note>. For when the **King** <note>original: *König*; a common alchemical code for gold.</note> is *reduced* <note>meaning broken down into its fundamental parts.</note> into his starting materials, and the *Sulfur or Soul of the Sun* <note>original: *Sulphur sive Anima Solis*; alchemists believed metals were composed of "Sulfur" (the soul/form) and "Mercury" (the spirit/body).</note> is at hand, then by the familiar **Philosophical Spirit** <note>likely referring to a specialized acidic solvent.</note> it must be *resolved* into an *Oil* **amicably, without noise or drawing [heat]** <note>original: *amicabiliter absque strepitu seu adductione*; meaning the chemical reaction should occur gently and naturally rather than through violent boiling or forced external heat.</note>, or heating, etc. *Hence, by means of the Spirit of Wine* <note>original: *Spiritu Vini*; concentrated alcohol.</note> *Potable Gold is made; otherwise, truly and even without it, a Tincture is made by means of Vulcan* <note>original: *per Vulcanum*; in alchemy, "Vulcan" is the personification of fire or the furnace.</note>, *but then the Salt of Gold must be present*, which is very easily obtained from a **dead body** <note>original: *todten Leib*; here referring to the "caput mortuum" or the useless dross left over after a substance has been distilled or sublimated.</note> from which the soul has departed; so that besides the starting materials, a **fourth thing** is to be seen, which I call the **earthly curse** and the *sinning matter, or the receptacle of its own evil and of another’s good* <note>original: *materia peccans, sive receptaculum mali proprii & boni alieni*; this refers to the impurities or "original sin" of the matter that must be discarded to reach the pure medicinal essence.</note>.

Where now, through such a separation, a **Tincture** is drawn from the *Solar Sulfur* by the *Universal Menstruum* <note>original: *Universal Menstruum*; the "great solvent" capable of dissolving all things into their primary essence.</note>, there, in all eternity, no *reduction* <note>meaning a reversal back into solid metal.</note> into the previous form can be brought about: For the malleable *Metallic Body* must have its preservation and malleability from its **Salt**; but here, there is only pure **Soul** and the *Spirit of Hermes* <note>original: *Spiritus Hermetis*; a reference to the volatile, spiritual essence associated with Hermes/Mercury.</note>, etc. **Therefore** [it is irreducible].

<summary>The author explains his retirement and directs readers to Jacob Hanssen in Cologne for his Potable Gold, concluding with a technical explanation of how the medicinal "Soul of Gold" is permanently separated from its "dead" metallic body.</summary>
<keywords>Potable Gold, Jacob Hanssen, Lion's Blood, Soul of the Sun, Vulcan, Salt of Gold, Universal Menstruum, Spirit of Hermes, alchemy, Cologne</keywords>

--- Page 148 ---
<meta>This page concludes the author's final instructions regarding the "Potable Gold" and offers a closing prayer. It follows the discussion on the previous page regarding the chemical reduction of gold into an oil using the "Spirit of Wine."</meta>

our solution or **Potable Gold** <note>original: *Aurum Potabile*</note> must be called irreducible <note>meaning the gold has been so fundamentally altered into a medicinal liquid that it can no longer be melted back into a solid metallic state.</note>, which I report here in truth: Therefore, may the kind reader believe me and not presume to obtain a **Tincture** <note>a "Tincture" in alchemy refers to a potent substance capable of transmuting base metals or healing the body; the author is clarifying that this specific preparation is a medicine, not a transmuting agent.</note> through this; but give back to the glorified **Fiery Souls** their own clarified **Body** and **Salt** <note>Alchemists believed all matter was composed of three parts: Soul (Sulfur), Spirit (Mercury), and Body (Salt). Here, he describes reuniting these purified components.</note>, and bring this missing **Spirit** back through the Salt into a state of hardening <note>solidification</note>, then the spiritual becomes corporeal, etc. But you will see what the Almighty will give to you, when you wait out the time with patience and caution; to Him alone you should attribute the glory, for I am nothing more than a calling voice in the desert world, which announces to you the attainment of Light, Wisdom, Riches, and Health. But the Light is the **Alpha and Omega** <note>Greek: "The Beginning and the End."</note>, the beginning and the end of all things.

> O Beginning without beginning, and End without end, grant to all those who have obtained the beginning of this Wisdom from You, a blessed effect, for Your glory and the salvation of their souls, so that those who have their beginning from You, the **Alpha**, may also henceforth enjoy the **Omega**, the End without end of Your infinite goodness with You in all eternity, and may love, praise, honor, and thank You. Amen.

->By love,<- <note>original: *Amore*</note>
->By custom,<- <note>original: *More*</note>
->By word,<- <note>original: *Ore*</note>
->By deed.<- <note>original: *Re*; this is a classic Latin wordplay where each subsequent word is contained within the previous one.</note>
